iṉip, paṉināṭukaḷil uḷḷārellām pulāluṇavē koḷkiṉṟāreṉpatum uṇmaiyuṇarātār veṟṟuraiyām. nammai yāḷum āṅkila naṉmakkaḷ vāḻum iṅkilāntu tēyattiṟku vaṭakkiṉkaṇ uḷḷatāṉa kāṭlāntu tēyam paṉiyuṅ kuḷirum mikkateṉpatu nila nūlaṟiñarum paḷḷikkūṭac ciṟuvaruṅkūṭa naṉkuṇarvar. annāṭṭiluḷḷa makkaḷ oṭ ṭariciyiṟ ceyta appamum uruḷaikkiḻaṅkum pālum veṇṇeyumē uṇavākak koḷḷukiṉṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai nilaiyaik kaṇṭuṇarnta āṭam mit (Adam smith) eṉṉum poruḷ nūl āciriyar, kacāppuk kaṭaikkāraṉ tarum iṟaicci ciṟitum illāmalē, vittukkaḷuṅ kāykaṟikaḷum, pāl veṇṇey pāṟkaṭṭi eṇṇey mutaliyaṉavuñ cērttu mikavuñ ceḻumaiyāṉa, mikavum nalantaruvatāṉa, mikavum uṭampai vaḷarppatāṉa, mikavum uṭampait tēṟṟuvatāṉa nalluṇavākumeṉpatu nām paḻakkattāṟ kaṇṭaṟintatākum eṉa moḻintirukkiṉṟār. airōppā amerikkā eṉṉun tēyaṅkaḷiṉ ūṭu ceṉṟu āṅkāṅku makkaḷ vāḻkkaiyiṉ iyalpukaḷai naṉku kaṇṭaṟinta avvarṭ mālkam (Rev Howard Malcolm) eṉṉum kiṟittu camaya kuru yāṉ kaṇṭa mika nērttiyāṉa makkaḷuṭampiṉ amaippukaḷil mikac ciṟantatu ayarlāntu tēyattiluḷḷa makkaḷiṉ uṭampēyākum; immakkaḷ orukālum ūṉuṇavaic cuvaittatēyillai eṉa moḻintirukkiṉṟār.3 ivvāṟu kuḷirum paṉikkaṭṭiyum niṟainta kāṭlāntu, ayarlāntu mutalāṉa nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa naṉmakkaḷ palaruñ caivavuṇavē uṭkoṇṭu uṭampiṟ cūṭuñ curucuruppum uṭaiyarāy iṉitu uyirvāḻntuvaral kaṇkūṭāy aṟiyappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, paṉi nāṭukaḷil uḷḷārkku ūṉuṇavu iṉṟiyamaiyātatu eṉṟu uraippār uṇmai yuṇarn tārāvarō? ciṟitum ākāreṉa viṭukka.
iṉi, nām uṇṇum uṇavilum parukum nīrilum eṇṇiṟanta ciṟṟuyirkaḷ nam kaṇkaḷukkup pulaṉākāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa; perukkukaṇṇāṭi (Microscope) yiṉutavi koṇṭu nōkkuvāmāyiṉ, avai ettaṉai kōṭik kaṇakkāy nam uṇavup poruḷkaḷilum pālilum nīrilum ēṉaiyavaṟṟilum niṟaintirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatai aṟiyalām; nām uḷḷiḻukkum mūccilum ettaṉaiyō kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa ciṟṟuyirkaḷ cērntu nammuṭampiṉ uṭcellukiṉṟaṉa; iṅṅaṉam ovvoru noṭiyum nām eṇṇiṟanta uyirkaḷaik kolaiceypavarkaḷāy irukkaiyil, iṟaicci tiṉpatu oṉṟutāṉā kolaik kuṟṟamākum? eṉṟu cilar viṉāvukiṉṟārkaḷ. namatu uyir vāḻkkaiyil nammāl vilakkakkūṭiya nikaḻccikaḷum nammāl vilakkakkūṭāta nikaḻccikaḷum eṉa iru pakuppukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. nammāl vilakkakkūṭiya nikaḻccikaḷiṟ kuṟṟamāvaṉa iruntāṟ pakuttaṟivuṭaiya nām avaṟṟai vilakki yoḻukak kaṭamaippaṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. nām ceyyum oru toḻil piṟavuyirkkut tuṉpan taruvateṉṟum, ataṉai vilakki yoḻukuvatu avvuyirkkum namakkum naṉmaiyaiyē tarumeṉṟum, ataṉai aṅṅaṉam vilakkiyoḻukuvatu nammāl eḷitiṟ ceyyakkūṭiyatē yāmeṉṟum nām naṉkaṟintirukkaiyil, nām aṟinta avvaṇṇam naṭavāmāyiṉ, atu nampāṟ peruṅkuṟṟamēyātal tiṇṇamaṉṟō? oruvariṭattuḷḷa oru kuṟṟattaiyeṭuttu maṟṟoruvariṭattu oruvaṉ pēcuvatai nām nāṭōṟum kāṇkiṉṟōm. iṅṅaṉam avaṉ pēcuvatāl avaraip piṟar iḻivāka eṇṇa ataṉāl avar uḷam varuntukiṉṟār; ākavē, puṟaṅkūṟutalāṉatu piṟarkkut tuṉpan taruvateṉpataip puṟaṅkūṟuvōṉ naṉkaṟintirukkiṉṟāṉ; puṟaṅkūṟuvōṉai makkaḷ ellārum aruvaruttuviṭutalāṟ, puṟaṅkūṟutal taṉakkut tīṅkutaruvateṉpatai avaṉum uṇarkiṉṟāṉ; ap puṟaṅkūṟutalai viṭṭup piṟarai uyarttup pēci ellārkkum iṉiyaṉāy oḻukutalē taṉakkum piṟarkkum naṉmaiyait taruvateṉpatum, avvāṟu nallavaṉāy naṭappatu eḷitiṟ ceyyak kūṭiyatēyāmeṉpatum avaṉ cevvaṉē aṟintum, avaṉ aṅṅaṉam aṟintapaṭiyē naṭavāṉāyiṉ, aḵtu avaṉmēṟ peruṅkuṟṟattaic cumattumaṉṟō? oruvarmēṟ piṟaroruvar puṟaṅkūṟuvatu vaḻakkamāyiruttalāṟ, puṟaṅkūṟuñ ceyal kuṟṟamākāteṉa evarēṉuṅ kūṟat tuṇivarō? tuṇiyāraṉṟē. atupōlavē, āṭu māṭu kōḻi kuruvi kokku kuyil mīṉ mutalāṉa ciṟṟuyirkaḷai avaṟṟiṉ iṟaiccikkāka makkaḷ kolai ceykaiyil, avai evvaḷavu tuṭituṭittut tuṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉa! aṅṅaṉam avai tuṉpuṟak koṉṟu peṟṟut tiṉṉum iṟaicciyāvatu taṉṉait tiṉpārkku nalantarukiṉṟatā? atuvumillaiyē! iṟaicci tiṉpārkku atu vayiṟṟuvali, malaccikkal, aḷavukku miñciya catai, irattakkuḻāykaḷiṟ koḻuppaṭaittal, kaikāṟpiṭippu, irumal, elumpurukki, kāmālai, piḷavaikkaṭṭi, nīriḻivu, koṟukku, paṭai, kaṭuṅ kāyccal, perumpāṭu mutalāka eṇṇat tolaiyāta nōykaḷai varicai varicaiyāyk koṭuttu avarai vakaimāḷac ceykiṉṟataṉṟō! iṅṅaṉamākat tāmum aḷavillāt tuṉpattaiyaṭaintu mēṟ kāṭṭiya ciṟṟuyirkaṭkun tuṉpattait tarutaṟku ētuvāṉa uyirkkolaiyiṉ tīmaiyai naṉku aṟintu vaittum, atu vaḻakkamāka uḷḷatutāṉē eṉṟu pakuttaṟi villā vilaṅkukaḷaip pōṟ pakuttaṟivuṭaiya makkaḷuñ ceyyalāmō? ittakaiya perum pollāṅkāṉa uyirkkolaiyaiyum pulāluṇavaiyum aṟavē nīkki, marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷiṉ payaṉāṉa caiva uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷai uṭkoṇṭu uyirvāḻtalāṟ piṟa vuyirkkum makkaḷākiya tamakkum evvaḷavu naṉmaiyuṇṭām! mēlum, pulāluṇavai nīkkuvatu varuttamāṉa ceyalum aṉṟu; aḵtu eḷitiṉ muṭivatēyām. ēṉeṉṟāl, caiva uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷaik kalavāmal iṟaicciyai maṭṭum vilaṅkukaḷaip pōl tiṉpatu makkaḷāṟ ciṟitum ēlātu; āṉāl, iṟaicciyillāmaṟ caiva uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷai maṭṭum arunti iṉitu uyirvāḻtalō makkaṭku eḷitilē icaivatākum; ataṟkāka mikunta poruṭcelavu ceyyavēṇṭuvatu millai. ivvāṟu eḷitiṟ kiṭaippatum, piṟavuyirkkut tīṅkillātatun, taṉṉai uṇpārkku mikka uṭal nala maṉanalaṅkaḷait taruvatum āṉa caivavuṇavai viṭuttu, aintaṟivu uyirkaḷait tuṭituṭikka kolluṅ kolait toḻilāl varum pulāluṇavai uṭkoḷḷutal pakuttaṟivuṭaiya makkaḷukkut takutiyāmō?
nām uṭkoḷḷuñ caiva uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷilum nīriluṅ kaṇakkaṟṟa ciṟṟuyirkaḷ nam kaṭpulaṉukkut teṉpaṭāmaliruntāl, ataṟku nām eṉ ceyyalām! nam maṉamārat terintavaikaḷaiyē nām kollāmal vilakki yoḻukak kaṭamaippaṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. namakkut teriyātavaikaḷaip paṟṟi, nammāl vilakkak kūṭātavaikaḷaip paṟṟik kavalaiyeṭuttalāl yātu payaṉuṇṭu? evarun tām aṟinta, tammāl vilakkak kūṭiya tīmaikaḷait tām tām vilakkalām; tammāl aṟiyappaṭāmalum, aṅṅaṉam aṟiyappaṭāmaiyāl tammāl vilakkak kūṭāmalum varum naṉmai tīmaikaḷukku evarum poṟuppāḷiyākamāṭṭār. tamakkut teriyāmalun tammāl virumpappaṭāmalun tam vayiṟṟiṉuḷḷē eṇṇiṟanta ciṟṟuyirkaḷ nuḻaivatu koṇṭu, āṭu māṭu mutalāṉa ciṟanta payaṉuḷḷa vilaṅkukaḷait tām vēṇṭiyapaṭi cennīr oḻukak koṉṟuṅ kolluvittum neñcāra avaṟṟiṉ ūṉai uṇpatu kuṟṟamākāteṉa enta aṟiñaṉēṉuṅ kūṟat tuṇivaṉō? marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷ puṟpūṇṭukaḷum uyir uṭaiyaṉa veṉpatu uṇmaiyē; āṉāl, iv ōraṟivuṭaiya uyirkaḷai nām uṇavākak koḷvatu kuṟṟamākātatu koṇṭu, aintaṟivuṭaiya āṭu māṭu mīṉ mutaliyavaikaḷai uṇavākak koḷvatuṅ kuṟṟamākātu eṉṉalāmō? āṭu māṭu mīṉ mutaliyavaikaḷai uṇṇātu nām uyirvāḻalām, āṉāl puṟpūṇṭukaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ payaṉkaḷaiyum uṇṇātu nām uyirvāḻtal kūṭumā? āṭu māṭu mutaliyavaikaḷaip piṟar kollap pukumpoḻutu, avai tam uyir tappippiḻaikka evvaḷavō muyaṉṟu tuṭituṭikkiṉṟaṉa! atupōṟ puṟpūṇṭu kaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ payaṉkaḷaiyum nām kaik koḷkaiyil, avaikaḷum tam uyir tappa muyaṉṟu ōṭukiṉṟaṉavā? ciṟitum illaiyē. āṭu māṭu mīṉ mutaliyavaikaḷaik koṉṟu kaikkoḷḷum iṟaicci, irattam oḻukit tīya muṭaināṟṟam vīcuvatupōṟ puṟpūṇṭu mutaliyavaikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ payaṉum irattañ cinti nāṟak kaṇṭatuṇṭō? īraṟivu mutal aintaṟivukāṟumuḷḷa ellāc ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṉ iṟaicciyum irattamum muṭai nāṟṟamum vīci aruvarukkat takkatāyirukka, ōraṟivuḷḷa puṟpūṇṭukaḷum avaṟṟiṉ payaṉāṉa ilai pū kāy kaṉi kiḻaṅku vittu mutaliyaṉavum evvaḷavu naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻntu nāvukkuc cuvaiyum uṭampukku nalamum uḷḷattiṟku makiḻcciyun tarukiṉṟaṉa! ākavē, ittaṉai vēṟupāṭumuṭaiya ūṉaiyuṅ kāykaṟiyuṇaviṉaiyum oṉṟeṉṟal pētaimaiyuṅ koṭumai yum irakkamaṟṟa vaṉṉeñcamum uṭaiyōr pulluraiyēyātal teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
mēlum, nammāl vilakka muṭiyāta cila ciṟu kuṟṟaṅkaḷ nammiṭam iruppatu paṟṟi, nammāl vilakkak kūṭiya peruṅkuṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum nām ceyyalām eṉṟu cerukkuṭaṉ kūṟuvār tam maṭamai peritum añcaṟpālatām. evvaḷavu naṟumaṇaṅkamaḻuñ cuvaippaṇṭaṅkaḷai nām uṇṭālum, avaiyellām nam vayiṟṟiṉ kīḻ aruvarukkattakka malamāy māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa; ivvāṟuṇṭākum malam namatu malakkuṭalil illāta nēramē illai. iṅṅaṉam makkaḷatu aṭivayiṟṟiṉuḷḷēyē malam iruttal koṇṭu makkaḷellōrum malattaiyē tiraḷaitiraḷaiyāka eṭuttuc cāppiṭalām eṉṟu kūṟa oruvar muṉvaruvarāyiṉ avaratu aṟivaiyum avaraiyum ulakattār eṉṉeṉṟu koḷvar! avarai aṟivu tirinta veṟiyareṉa ikaḻntu nakaipparallarō! ettaṉai muṟai kuḷittālum namatuṭampil aḻukku iruttalāl, nām kuḷittalē vēṇṭāmeṉak kūṟalāmō? aṅṅaṉamē, nām ettaṉai muṟai ettaṉai nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟālum, nām avaṟṟiṉ coṟporuḷkaḷai maṟantuviṭutalāl, nām kalvikaṟṟalē vēṇṭāmeṉak karaiyalāmō? iṅṅaṉamellām nammaṉō riṭattil vilakkamuṭiyāta cila ciṟu kuṟṟaṅkaḷ iruppatu koṇṭu, avarkaḷ peruṅkuṟṟaṅkaḷaic ceyyalāmeṉavum appaṭic ceyvatu kuṟṟamākātu eṉavum pakara evarēṉum oruppaṭuvarō? oruppaṭāraṉṟē! avai pōlavē, nām aṟiyāmalum nām virumpāmalum irukka, nam kaṭpulaṉukku akappaṭāta nuṇṇiya ciṟṟuyirkaḷ pala nām uṇṇuñ caiva uṇavupporuḷkaḷiluṅ kalantu nammāl vilakka muṭiyātapaṭi nam vayiṟṟuṉuṭ celvatu kuṟittu, nampōl aintaṟivum uṭampiṉ amaippum vāynta āṭu māṭu kōḻi kokku mīṉ mutalāṉa ciṟṟuyirkaḷaiyum nām nam neñcāra virumpik koṉṟum kolvittum avaṟṟiṉ ūṉait tiṉpamāṉāl, atu maṉṉikkappaṭāta peruṅkuṟṟamāy muṭiyumeṉṟu uṇarntukoḷka.
aḵtu uṇmaiyēyeṉṟāluñ, ciṟṟuyirkaḷilēyē oṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟaip piṭittut tiṉṟu uyirvāḻtal iyaṟkai nikaḻcciyāyk kāṇappaṭṭu varutalāl, makkaḷum piṟa ciṟṟuyirkaḷaik koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉai uṇpatu kuṟṟamākāteṉiṉ; naṉṟu coṉṉāy; ciṟṟuyirkaḷ oṉṟaiyoṉṟu piṭittut tiṉṉal pōla, makkaḷun tammuḷ oruvaraiyoruvar koṉṟu tiṉṉal vēṇṭumeṉak kūṟutal vēṇṭum. kāṭṭumirāṇṭi vakuppiṉar cilar tammuḷḷē oruvaraiyoruvar koṉṟu tiṉṟuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉamākap pakuttaṟivillāk kāṭṭumirāṇṭikaḷ ceyyuṅ koṭuñ ceyalaip pārttup pakuttaṟivuṭaiya makkaḷum avvāṟu ceytal iyaṟkai tāṉeṉṟu collit, tammuḷ oruvaraiyoruvar koṉṟu tiṉṉalāmō? atuvēyumaṉṟic, ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyē iyaṟkaikku ottateṉṟu karuti, ataṉpaṭi naṭakkap pukuntāl, makkaḷ vāḻkkaiyāṉatu tāḻnta kāṭṭuvilaṅkukaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyāy, ūrellāṅ kāṭukaḷāy māṟal vēṇṭum. vilaṅkukaḷaippōl nākarika makkaḷum āṭaiyiṉṟi ammaṇamāy ulavutal vēṇṭum; avaikaḷ vīṭuvāytaliṉṟi maraṅkaḷiṉ kīḻum malaikkukaikaḷiṉuḷḷun taṅkiyiruttalpōla avarkaḷum vīṭuvāytalait tuṟantu maraṅkaḷ malaik kukaikaḷil taṅkal vēṇṭum; avaikaḷuṭ cila maṟṟa uyirkaḷai aṭittuk koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ cataiyaip paccaiyāyk kiḻittuṇṭal pōla, avarkaḷum vilaṅkukaḷaik koṉṟu paccaiyāka avaṟṟiṉ iṟaicciyait tiṉṉal vēṇṭum; avaikaḷuṭ paṉṟi mutalāṉa cila vilaṅkukaḷ malattaic cāppiṭutal pōla, avarkaḷum malattai viḻuṅkutal vēṇṭum; avaikaḷellām kalvi kaṟkavum kaṭavuḷai vaṇaṅkavum aṟiyāmaipōla, avarkaḷuṅ kalvi kallāmaluṅ kaṭavuḷai vaṇaṅkāmaluṅ kālaṅ kaḻittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamellām vilaṅkiṉ iyaṟkaikaḷōṭu oppa, nākarika makkaḷum nākarika vāḻkkaiyaiviṭṭu uyir vāḻal kūṭumāyiṉaṉṟō, avarkaḷ avaikaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟaippōl ūṉtiṉṟu uyir vāḻtal poruntuvatākumā? nākarika makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyāṉatu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ vāḻkkaikku muṟṟum māṟupaṭṭatāyirukkaiyil, ūṉ tiṉpataṟku maṭṭum avaṟṟiṉ iyaṟkaiyai avarkaḷ piṉpaṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉac ciṟitum ārāyātu kūṟuvār kūṟṟiṉ maṭamaip perukkai eṉṉeṉṟu pukalvēm! aṟiñarkaḷē āḻntu pārmiṉkaḷ!
iṉit, talaiyiṟ puḻukkum pēṉkaḷaiyun, talaiyaṇai pāy paṭukkai mūṭṭukkaḷiṟ puḻukkum mūṭṭup pūccikaḷaiyum, mōṭṭuvaḷaiyiluḷḷa elikaḷaiyum makkaḷ kolla vēṇṭiyavarkaḷāy iruttalāl, avarkaḷ tam uṭampu vaḷarcciyiṉ poruṭṭu uyirkaḷaik kolla vēṇṭiyavarkaḷā yirukkiṉṟaṉareṉap pulāluṇavu koḷvār cilar pukalkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ tamatu nalattiṉ poruṭṭut tammōṭotta makkaḷaik koṉṟuviṭutaluṅ kuṟṟamākāteṉac colvataṟkup piṉvāṅkār. aruḷoḻukkamuṭaiyarāy uyir vāḻa virumpum mēṉ makkaḷō ittakaiya koṭiyōr colluṅ koṭuñ coṟkaḷukkuc ciṟituñ cevi koṭuttalākātu. pēṉ, mūṭṭuppūcci, eli mutalāṉavaikaḷ nām irukkum illaṅkaḷil uṇṭātaṟkum varutaṟkum iṭañ ceyyāmal iruppamāyiṉ, avaṟṟai nām kolla vēṇṭuvatum nērātu. talaiyilum āṭaiyilum aḻukkuppiṭiyātapaṭi nīrāṭik kaḻuvit tuppuravāy iruppamāṉāl, pēṉ uṇṭāka māṭṭātu; paṭukkaikaḷaiyum aṅṅaṉamē aḻukkum īramum aṇukāmal veyilil aṭuttaṭuttuk kāya vaittut tūyaṉavāy vaittuk koṇṭāl mūṭṭup pūcci uṇṭākātu; illattuḷḷa aṟaikaḷil veḷiccamum kāṟṟum mikutiyāy ulavumpaṭi ceytu, iṇṭuiṭukkukaḷ vaḷaikaḷ illāmal aṭaittu, uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷaip pēḻaikaḷilum nilaippēḻaikaḷilum vaittu mūṭik karuttāy naṭantāl elikaḷ varamāṭṭā. aṉṟi, avvāṟellām karuttāy iruntum, orōvorukāl nam illaṅkaḷuṭpukuntu nammai varutti nam uyirkkut tīṅku ceyyumāyiṉ, appōtu avaṟṟai nammiṉiṉṟum akaṟṟutal kuṟṟamākātu. kaṭikka varum pāmpiṉaiyuṅ kolla varum puliyiṉaiyuṅ koṉṟu nammainām pātukāttal kuṟṟamākātatu pōlavum, namatuṇavukku iṉṟiyamaiyāta neṟpayiraip pātukāttaṟ poruṭṭu avaṟṟiṭaiyē vaḷarntu avaṟṟai yaḻikkum palvakaip pūṇṭukaḷaik kaḷainteṟital kuṟṟamākātatu pōlavum, nammuyirkkut tīṅku tarum entac ciṟṟuyirkaḷaiyum nammiṉiṉṟum akaṟṟutal ciṟituṅ kuṟṟamākā teṉka. eṉṟālum, ac ciṟṟuyirkaḷai nammiṉiṉṟu akaṟṟumiṭattum, avai tammai nammāṟ kūṭiyavaraiyiṟ kollāmal akaṟṟutalē nām irakka neñcamuṭaiyarāy oḻukutaṟkuc ciṟanta neṟiyām eṉpatai aṉaivarum neñciṟ patittal vēṇṭum. vilakamuṭiyātapaṭi avaṟṟai aḻittu akaṟṟa nērntapaḻi, avai tīṅkilāta piṟaviyiṟ cērumpaṭi iṟaivaṉai vēṇṭi maṉṉippukkēṭṭu avaṟṟai avvāṟu ceytal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamākac cila ciṟṟuyirkaḷai vilakka muṭiyāta paṭi cila nēraṅkaḷil makkaḷ aḻittuviṭa vēṇṭiyiruppatākiya oru pulliya kāraṇattaik koṇṭu, avarkaḷ āṭu māṭu kōḻi kokku mīṉ mutalāṉa tīṅkaṟṟa uyirkaḷaiyuṅ koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉai uṇṇalāmeṉṟu muṉpiṉ ciṟitum irakkamillāmaṟ pēcum vaṉṉeñcarkaḷiṉ koṭumaiyai eṉṉeṉpēm! pulāluṇpavar mūṭṭup pūccik kāraṇaṅ koṇṭu pulāluṇṇalām eṉṟu aḻivaḻakkup pēcuvā rāyiṉ, avar ammūṭṭuppūcci pēṉ kocuku mutalāṉavai kaḷaiyun tiṉṉāmai eṉṉaiyōveṉṟu kūṟiviṭukka.
iṉi, nam intiya nāṭṭavarai ayalnāṭṭavariṉ aṭimaittaṉat tiṉiṉṟum mīṭka muyalvār pōl āravārañ ceyvār cilar. nammaṉōrkkup pōr puriyum āṟṟalum āṇmaittaṉmaiyum uṇṭākal vēṇṭumāyiṉ, avar kaḷellārum pulāluṇavu koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉa evaruṅ kāṇāp putiyatoru vaḻiyaik kaṇṭupiṭittuc colli varukiṉṟār. ivarkaḷ, kaṭavuḷaiyum illaiyeṉṟu tuṇivāyc collum pāḻṅkoḷkai yuṭaiyavarkaḷātalāl, ivarkaḷ tammaiyotta makkaḷoṭu pōrpurintu avarkaḷaip pāḻpaṭuttaṟkut, tīṅkaṟṟa uyirkaḷaiyum pāḻpaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūvukiṉṟaṉar! puli karaṭi ōnāyaip pōṟ piṟa uyirkaḷaik kollutaṟku viḻaintu muṉ niṟkum ittakaiya tīviṉaiyāḷar irukkuṅkāṟum ulakil amaitiyum naṉmaiyum irā. ik koṭiyavar tokai perukumāyiṉ in nilam veṟuñ cuṭu kāṭāyviṭum! āṉāl, ellā irakkamum ellā vallamaiyum uṭaiya āṇṭavaṉ ik koṭiyavar tokaiyaip peruka viṭāṉ eṉpatu tiṇṇam. ēṉeṉṟāṟ, kolait toḻililum pulāluṇṇutalilum mutaṉmai peṟṟu niṉṟa mēṉāṭṭavar kaḷilēyē aṟivum ārāycciyum mutirnta mēṉmakkaḷ pallāyirakkaṇakkāy nāḷukkunāṭperukik, kolait toḻilaiyum ataṉāl varum ūṉ uṇṭalaiyum peruntīṅkeṉa uṇarntu, avaṟṟai maṟuttal iṉṟiyamaiyāta teṉap pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa nūlkaḷālun tuṇṭu viḷamparaṅkaḷālum makkaḷukku aṟivupukaṭṭi varukiṉṟārkaḷ. ellāruṅkāṇa avarkaḷ iṭaiviṭātu ceytuvarum ārāyccikaḷi ṉutavikoṇṭu, ūṉuṇpārkku nōykaḷ pala uṇṭāki avartam uṭal nalattai aḻittalāl avarkku uṭalāṟṟal maṉavāṟṟalkaḷ illaiyām; avai illaiyākavē avar āṇmaittaṉmai yuṭaiyavarātalum illaiyām eṉṟum, marakkaṟiyuṇavu koḷpavarkku uṭalnala maṉanalaṅkaḷ tāmākavē amaitalāl avar iyaṟkaiyākavē āṇmaiyum amaitittaṉmaiyum uṭaiyarāy ettakaiya arumperu muyaṟciyaiyuñ ceytu muṭikka vallunarāyirukkiṉṟaṉareṉṟum pulāluṇṭaliṉ tīmaiyaiyum pulāl maṟuttaliṉ naṉmaiyaiyum naṉku viḷakkivarukiṉṟaṉar. am mēṉmakkaḷ ceytupōtarum ārāyccikaḷiṟ cila muṉṉarē eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟēm.
mēluṅ, koṭumaiyuñ ciṉamum illā amaitiyuṭaiyārkkē āṟṟalum aṟivum mikutippaṭa, avarkaḷē tamakkum piṟarkkum payaṉpaṭṭu oḻukuvareṉpatum, ūṉuṇṭu koṭumaiyuñ ciṉamum aḷavupaṭāmal mikap peṟṟavar tamakkum piṟarkkum payaṉilarātaluṭaṉ, potu makkaḷiṉ iṉiya vāḻkkaiyaiyuṅ kulaittuviṭu nīrarātalaiyum nāṭōṟum āṅkāṅku nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷil vaittu naṉkaṟiyalām. aṭipiṭi caṇṭaiyil muṉaintu niṟpavarkaḷum, muṉpiṉpārāmal āṟaṟivuṭaiya makkaḷaic cennīr oḻukat tuṭituṭikka veṭṭikkolpavarkaḷum yāvareṉa ārāyntu pārkkac ciṟṟuyirk kolaiyait toḻilākak koṇṭu ūṉ viṟpavarkaḷum pulāluṇavu nirampak koḷpavarkaḷumē ak koṭun toḻilaic ceypavarāyirukkiṉṟaṉareṉa uṇavārāycciyiṟ ṟērnta aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇakkeṭuttiruk kiṉṟārkaḷ. avarpōl attuṇai nuṇṇiya ārāycci ceyyamāṭṭāta nām, nammiṉun tāḻnta vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iyaṟkaiyil vaittu ivvuṇmaiyai eḷitāka aṟintu koḷḷalām. ūṉaiyē tiṉṟu uyir vāḻum puli karaṭi ciṅkam ōnāy mutalāṉa maṟavilaṅkukaḷiṉ iyaṟkaiyaiyum, marattiṉ kuḻaikaḷaiyum pullaiyum vaikkōlaiyumē tiṉṟu uyirvāḻum āṭu māṭu erumai kutirai kaḻutai yāṉai oṭṭakam mutalāṉa aṟavilaṅkukaḷiṉ iyaṟkaiyaiyum pakuttup pārmiṉkaḷ! ivviruvakai vilaṅkukaḷil evai valimaiyiṟ ciṟantaṉa? puli mutaliyaṉavē valimai mikkaṉaveṉṟu nīṅkaḷ niṉaikkalām; āṉāl, aḵtu uṇmaiyaṉṟu; peruñcumai yēṟṟiya oru pāravaṇṭiyil iraṇṭu erutukaḷaiyum aṅṅaṉamē cumaiyēṟṟiya maṟṟoru vaṇṭiyil iraṇṭu pulikaḷaiyum pūṭṭi avaṟṟai ōṭṭiṉāl, erutukaḷaippōṟ pulikaḷ ataṉai nīṇṭavaḻi iḻuttuc cellumā? ciṟituñ cellā. maṟavilaṅkukaṭkuk kūriya nakaṅkaḷum paṟkaḷum iruttal paṟṟi avai valimaiyuṭaiyaṉa pōṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavē yallāmal, māṭu kutirai yāṉai mutaliya aṟavilaṅkukaḷaip pōl avai valimaiyiṟ ciṟantaṉa alla. atuvēyumaṉṟi, māṭu kutirai mutalāṉavaikaḷaip pōṟ puli karaṭi mutalāyiṉa makkaṭkuc ciṟitēṉum payaṉpaṭuvatum illai. ivvāṟu ivviruvēṟu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iyaṟkaikaḷaiyum uṟṟu nōkku talālēyē pulāluṇpavar tamakkum piṟarkkum payaṉpaṭattakka, āṟṟalum aṟivum ilarātal eḷitil viḷaṅkum. it tuṇaitāṉum āyntu pārāmaṟ pēcuvārtam veṟṟuraiyiṉai nampip, pulāluṇpārkkē, aṟivum āṇmaiyum uṇṭāmeṉa eṇṇiviṭātīrkaḷ!
iṉi, iññāṉṟuḷḷa makkaṭpakuppiṉar cilarkkuḷḷa āṇmaittaṉmaiyiṉ kāraṇam yātu? eṉṟu ārāyntu pārppīrkaḷāyiṉ, aḵtu avar ōvātu ceytu pōtarum aṟivārāycciyē yāvatallāl avarkkuḷḷa uṭal valimai yaṉṟeṉpatūun teḷiya uṇarvīrkaḷ. muṉṉāḷil aracar iruvarkkuṭ pōr mūṇṭāl, avviruvarkkuriya paṭai makkaḷum oruvarōṭoruvar neruṅkip pōr purintu oruvaraiyoruvar veṭṭic cāyttu, irutiṟattārum peruntuṉpam eytuvar. maṟṟu, innāḷilō pattukkal irupatukal vilaki niṉṟu irupaṭaikaḷum pōrpuriyattakka palatiṟak karuvikaḷ aṟiñarkaḷāṟ putitu kaṇṭupiṭittu amaikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. pattukkal irupatu kaṟkaḷukku appāl niṟkum paṭaikaḷaiyuñ cuṭṭuc cāykkum pīraṅkikaḷum, aimpatukal aṟupatu kaṟkaḷukku appāl niṟkum paṭaikaḷaiyum araimaṇi nērattiṟ paṟantuceṉṟu māykkum vāṉakkappalkaḷum, nīr mēl niṟkuñ caṇṭaikkappalkaḷai evarum aṟiyāmal nīruḷ mūḻkic ceṉṟu takarkkum nīrmūḻkik kappalkaḷum, nam collaḷavil aṭaṅkā eṉṉum ettaṉaiyō arumperuṅ karuvikaḷum ikkālattiṟ putiyaputiyavāyk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu nilavukiṉṟaṉavē! avaiyellām uṭal valimaiyāl ākkappaṭṭaṉavā? aṉṟi aṟivu valimaiyāl ākkap paṭṭaṉavā? ellām aṟivu valimaiyāl allavō ākkap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟu ciṟu makāruñ colvar! iññāṉṟu putumaiyiṉum putumaiyāyk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa vellām ūṉ valimai yuṭaiyavarkaḷāl vantaṉa alla; maṟṟup paḻamum pālum vittukkaḷumākiya ciṟṟuṇā uṇṭu, iravil iraṇṭu maṇinēraṅkūṭa uṟaṅkāta aṟiñar perumakkaḷālēyē kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu, avartam varalāṟukaḷai uṇarvār naṉkaṟivar. miṉcāra āṟṟaliṉ vakaimaikaḷai ulakamellām viyakkumpaṭi putiya putiyavāyk kaṇṭupiṭittu viḷaṅkum eṭicaṉ eṉṉum aṟiñarperumāṉ varalāṟē itaṟkuc cāṉṟām. ākavē, aṟivuvalimai paṭaittārkkē ellā valimaiyum uṇṭākumallāmal, vēṟu ūṉ potitāṅ kiṉārkku ētoru valimaiyum uṇṭākāteṉṟuṇarntu koḷka. itupaṟṟiyē āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
aṟivuṭaiyār ellām uṭaiyār aṟivilār
eṉṉuṭaiya rēṉum ilar	(kuṟaḷ 430)
eṉṟatūum, paḻaiya caṅkakālattup pulavar oruvar,
ūṉaittiṉ ṟūṉaip perukkāmai muṉ iṉitē 
eṉṟatūu meṉka.
iṉik, kaṇṇappanāyaṉār tāmun tiṉṟu iṟaivaṉukkum paṭaitta vilaṅkiṉ iṟaicciyai iṟaivaṉ ēṟṟuk koṇṭamai, avartam varalāṟu kūṟum nūlkaḷālum pāṭṭukkaḷālum naṉkaṟiyakkiṭattaliṉ, ūṉ uṇpatu iṟaivaṉ karuttukku māṟākāteṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar. ivar kūṟṟup porutta muṭait takāmaiyiṉaic ciṟitu viḷakkuvām; kaṇṇappa nāyaṉārō vēṭṭuva kulattiṟ piṟantu, ak kulattiṟkuriya vēṭṭaiyāṭun toḻililum, at toḻilāl varum ūṉai uṇavākak koḷḷum vāḻkkaiyilum paḻakiṉavar; vēṭṭa māṭun toḻil vilaṅkukaḷukkup peruntuṉpan taruvateṉpatu, ākavē vilaṅkukaḷaik koṉṟu peṟṟa iṟaicciyai iṟaivaṉukkup paṭaittal kuṟṟameṉpatuñ ciṟitum aṟiyātavar. ittaṉmaiyarākiya avar, mēṟpiṟavikaḷiṟ ceyta peruntavattiṉ muṭinta payaṉāy, ellāmvalla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa civapirāṉiṭattil mutirnta aṉpu koḷḷap peṟṟār. oruvarpāl aḷavukaṭanta aṉpu koḷḷappeṟṟa piṟaroruvar, tamakkuriya ciṟanta poruḷkaḷaiyum tām nukarum ciṟanta uṇavuppaṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum tamakkeṉa varaintu vaittuk koḷḷātu, tām aṉpu vaittavarkkē avai tammai yellāṅ koṭuttu, avar nukarntu miñciya miccattaiyē amiḻtiṉuñ ciṟantatākak karuti makiḻntu tām uṭkoḷḷā niṟpar. iḵtu ulaka vaḻakkilum nūl vaḻakkilum vaittuc cevvaṉē aṟiyappaṭuvatōr arumpēruṇmaiyām. ivvuṇmaikku icaiyavē, kaṇṇappa nāyaṉār tamakkuc ciṟanta uṇavākat tām karutiya muḷḷampaṉṟi māṉ marai kaṭampai mutalāṉa vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟaicciyai mutalil iṟaivaṉukkup paṭaittu, ataṉpiṉ, mikuntatait tām ayiṉṟu, iṟaivaṉatu aruṭ kuṟiyiṉai akalātu uṭaṉiruntu aṉpiṉāl akaṅkaraintu nīrāy urukiṉār. empāl aṉpu mītūrap peṟṟār oruvar, emakkuḷḷa aṟivu illārāy, yām virumpuvatu iṉṉa teṉṟum uṇarārāyt, tām ciṟantatākak kaikkoṇṭa oru paṇṭam evvaḷavu emmāl aruvarukkap paṭuvatāyiṉum ataṉait tām aṟiyāmē, empāl ataṉai nekkurukum neñcattōṭum uyttu, ataṉai ēṟṟaruḷka eṉa emmaik kuḻaintu kuḻainturuki vēṇṭiṭuvarāyiṉ; attuṇai aṉpuṭaiya avaratu vēṇṭukōḷai yām ēlātiruttal iyalumō! iyalātaṉṟē. ciṟṟaṟivuñ ciṟu toḻilumuṭaiya em pōṉṟārkkē, pēraṉpuṭaiyār ceyyum aṉpiṉ varicaikaḷai ēlāmal maṟuttal iyalāteṉṟāl, aṉpiṉ uruvāyt tikaḻum āṇṭavaṉ taṉṉaṭiyavar taṉpāl vaitta aṉpiṉ perukkāṟ ceyyum aṉpiṉ aṭaiyāḷac ceyalkaḷai - avai aṉpillāta ēṉai ulakattavarkku ettuṇai iḻintaṉavāka, ettuṇai icaiyātaṉavākak kāṇappaṭiṉum, ēlātoḻivaṉō? taṉpāl aṉpu mikka taṉ iṉiya makavu taṉ kaiyiṟ kiṭaitta ōr aruvaruppāṉa paṇṭattait taṉ vāyil vaittuṇṇumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttukaiyil, tāyāṉavar evvaḷavu makiḻvōṭu ataṉai vāṅki, aḵtu iḻintatēyāyiṉum uṇṇat takkatāyiruntāl uṇṭu viṭukiṉṟāḷ! uṇṇat takātatāyiruntāl ataṉai uṇpatu pōṟ kāṭṭi ataṉai appuṟam maṟaittu am makaviṉai makiḻvittut tāṉum makiḻkiṉṟāḷ. iṅṅaṉamē, aṉpiṉ perukkāṟ kaṇṇappa nāyaṉār paṭaitta ūṉ uṇā, iṟaivaṉāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭun takutiyuṭaittaṉṟēṉum, avaratu pēraṉpiṉ peṟṟi nōkki, avaṉāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatupōl tōṉṟalāyiṟṟu. ākavē, aṉpiṉuruvāṉa aiyaṉ aṭiyārkku eḷiyaṉān taṉmai yaṉātalāl, avaṉ avar poruṭṭuc ceypavaikaḷai yellām avaṉṟaṉ tiruvuḷattiṟku oppaṉavākak kūṟutal aṭāteṉṟuṇarka. tāykku ākātavaikaḷaik kuḻantai āvaṉavākak karuti aṉpiṉāṟ ceyyumāyiṉ, avai tammait tāyāṉavaḷ taṉakkum āmeṉak koṇṭaṉaḷeṉak kūṟutal okkumō, uraimiṉkaḷ! aṉparkaḷē! atupōlavē, kaṭavuḷun tamatu tiruvuḷattukku icaiyātavaikaḷai āmeṉak karutit tam aṉparkaḷ karaikaṭanta aṉpiṉ perukkāṟ ceykuvarāyiṉ, avaitammai āṇṭavaṉ aruṇmikutiyāl ēṟpatupōṟ kāṭṭi avartammai makiḻvippaṉ eṉṟuṇartal vēṇṭum.
aḵtokkumāyiṉum, aṉpiṉ mutircciyāṟ kaṇṇappar paṭaitta pulālai iṟaivaṉ uṇmaiyil ēṟṟaṉaṉ allaṉ eṉpataṟkum, pulāluṇavu koṇṭamaiyāṟ kaṇṇapparkku uṇṭāṉa tīviṉaiyaik kaḻittē iṟaivaṉ avarait taṉ tiruvaṭikkaṇ ēṟṟaruḷiṉaṉ eṉpataṟkum aṭaiyāḷam uḷatōveṉiṟ, kāṭṭutum: piṟa uyirkkuk kaṭuntuṉpattait taruṅ kolait toḻilaic ciṟu piḷḷaiyāyirunta kālan toṭṭē paḻaki vantavarkkut, tām ceyyum attoḻilāl avvuyirkaḷ evvaḷavu tuṉpattai aṭaikiṉṟaṉa  eṉṉum uṇarcci ciṟitum uṇṭākātu. iṅṅaṉat tām ciṟitum uṇarātē ak kolait toḻilai vaḻakkamāyc ceytu varukuvarēṉum, akkoṭun toḻilāl varuntīviṉai avarai viṭṭakalātu; piṟavikaṭōṟum avarait toṭarntu ceṉṟu palavāṟṟāṉum avarait tuṉpuṟuttāniṟkum. ittakaiyōr iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭip pēriṉpattaip peṟutaṟkuriya pēraṉpu mikap peṟṟārāṉālum, avartām ceytu pōnta kolait toḻilāṟ piṟa uyirukkuṇṭāṉa koṭuntuṉpattai uṇarntu, tāmum avvuyirpōṟ koṭuntuṉpam uḻantu tuṉpattiṟkut tuṉpam īṭukoṭuttāl allāmal, avar tammaic cūḻntu koṇṭirukkun tīviṉaiyai yoḻittuc civattiṉ aruḷaip peṟamāṭṭār. ātalāṟ, kaṇṇappa nāyaṉār kaṭavuḷiṭattu aḷavupaṭāp pēraṉpu koṇṭārāyiṉum, avar atukāṟuñ ceytu pōnta kolait toḻilāṟ piṟavuyirkkuṇṭāṉa peruntuṉpattiṉ aḷavaiyuṇarntu, at tuṉpattiṟkut tāmum perun tuṉpam eyti īṭucelutta vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatāyiṟṟu; avvāṟu ceytāṟṟāṉ avaraik kavinta peruntīviṉai akalum; at tīviṉai akaṉṟāṟṟāṉ avar civattiṉ aruḷait talaik kūṭutal vāykkum. āṉatu paṟṟiyē, ellām valla iṟaivaṉ kaṇṇappariṉ pēraṉpai ulakattārkkup pulappaṭuttal vēṇṭiyum avar atukāṟuñ ceytuvanta kolait toḻilāl viḷainta tīviṉaiyaik kaḻittal vēṇṭiyum taṉ tiruvuruvattiṉ valatu kaṇṇil nōy mikuntu nīr vaṭivatupōṟ kāṭṭiṉāṉ. ataṉaik kaṇṭa kaṇṇappar ‘emperumāṉ ṟaṉ kaṇṇukku vanta innōyai eṅṅaṉan tīrppēṉ! eṉṟu eṇṇiyeṇṇi ēṅki āṟṟārāyiṉār. muṭivāka, avarirunta vēṭṭuvavāḻkkaiyil, uṇavāka iṭutaṟkum nōy tīrttaṟkum urittāvatu iṉṉatu tāṉ eṉpatu pulaṉāyiṟṟu; tāmun tammaiyotta maṟṟa vēṭṭuvar uṭampum ūṉāṟ camaintavaiyātalāl, ūṉukku ūṉaiyiṭutalē paci tīrttaṟkum nōy tīrttaṟkum icainta muṟaiyākum eṉṟu kaṇṭaṟintār. avaratu vēṭṭuva vāḻkkaiyiṉ aḷavukku avaratu aṟivu kaṇṭa muṟai at tuṇaiyētāṉ. avvaṟivu tōṉṟiyavuṭaṉ avaraṭainta makiḻccikku ōr ellaiyē illai. emperumāṉukku vanta ik kaṇṇōyait tīrkka eṉ kaṇkaḷil oṉṟaip piṭuṅki, nōykaṇṭa kaṇṇil appak kaṭavēṉ eṉṟu maṉantuṇintu eḻuntu, āṇṭavaṉ tiruvuruvattiṉ arukē ceṉṟu, tamatu oru kūriya ampiṉ muṉaiyāl tamatu oru kaṇṇai aṭiyōṭu kalliyeṭukkalāṉār. pāruṅkaḷ, aṉparkaḷē! innērattil avar tam uṭampum uyirum evvaḷavu tuṉpuṟṟut tuṭituṭittirukkum! piṟavuyirai avaṟṟiṉ ūṉiṉ poruṭṭuk kūrṅ kaṇaiyāṟ kuttikkoṉṟa kālaṅkaḷil, avai evvaḷavu tuṉpuṟṟut tuṭituṭittirukkum! avvaḷavu tuṉpattiṟkum ippōtu kaṇṇappar tamatu kaṇṇaik kuttik kalli eṭukkiṉṟakālaiyil tāmum tuṉpuṟṟu tuṭituṭittu īṭu koṭuttavarāyiṉār! aiyaṟivuṭaiya ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṉ tuṉpa uṇarcciyāṉatu, āṟaṟivuṭaiya makkaḷiṉ tuṉpa uṇarcciyiṉum palapaṭi kuṟaintatu. ivvēṟupāṭu veṭṭuṇṭa ōr āṭṭiṉ uṭampukkuḷḷa tuṭituṭippaiyum aṅṅaṉamē veṭṭuṇṭa ōr āṇmakaṉuṭampukkuḷḷa āṟṟoṇāt tuṭituṭippaiyum kaṇṭa oruvarkkē teriyum; mēluñ, ciṟṟuyirkaḷāvatu piṟar kaiyāṟ kollappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; iṅkuk kaṇṇapparō tamatu kaiyāṟ ṟam kaṇṇaik kiṇṭi eṭukkiṉṟār! iḵtu evvaḷavu koṭuntuṉpam! atuvēyumaṉṟi, oruvaṉ taṉ kaḻuttait tāṉākavē viraintu veṭṭik koḷvāṉāyiṉ, avaṉṟaṉ uyirum uṇarvum viraivil avaṉatu uṭampaiviṭṭu akalum! ataṉāl tuṉpa uṇarcci neṭuka irātu; āṉāl, iṅkuk kaṇṇapparō tamatu uṭampiṉkaṇ tam uyirum uṇarvum nilaipeṟṟirukkum nilaiyil tamatu kaṇṇait tōṇṭi yeṭukkiṉṟār; ā! ītu evvaḷavu toṭarpāṉa tuṉpattait taruñceyal! ivvaḷavu koṭun tuṉpattaiyum poṟuttuk koṇṭu, avar kaṭavuḷpāṟ kāṭṭiya pēraṉpiṉ tiṟam pulliya em oru nāvāl viḷampalān taṉmaittō! ivvaḷavilāvatu civapirāṉ avarai viṭṭaṉarā! illai! illai! kaṇṇappar tamatu oru kaṇṇaip piṭuṅki appiya vuṭaṉ, civapirāṉ tamatu oru kaṇṇiliruntu nōy tīrntu viṭṭa aṭaiyāḷaṅ kāṭṭiṉāṉ. atu kaṇṭu kaṇṇappar meymmaṟanta kaḷippuṭaiyarāy āṭiṉār; pāṭiṉār! āṉāl, maṟṟaināḷ iṟaivaṉṟaṉ maṟṟoru kaṇṇilum nōymikuntu atiliruntum nīrvaṭiyalāyiṟṟu. ippōtu kaṇṇapparkku atu tīrkkum maruntu tammiṭam iruppatu naṉku teriyumātalāl, atupaṟṟi mikutiyāyk kavalai koṇṭilar. uṭaṉē tamatu maṟṟoru kaṇṇaiyun tōṇṭi eṭukka muṉaintaṉar. āṉāl, eñciyuḷḷa ivvoru kaṇṇaiyuṅ kalli yeṭuttuviṭṭāl, iṟaivaṉuruvattil uḷḷa kaṇṇil atai appuvataṟkut tamakkup pārvai teriyātē eṉṟu kalaṅkiṉār. ataṉpiṉ, tamatu kāṟperuviralai iṟaivaṉuruvil nōykaṇṭa kaṇṇiṉ arukē aṭaiyāḷamāka vaittuk koṇṭāl tām piṭuṅkum maṟṟak kaṇṇaiyum atil appiviṭalāmeṉṟu vaḻi terintār. terintatum akaṅkaḷittut tamatu iṭatu kāṟ  peruviralai aṅṅaṉamē aṭaiyāḷamāka vaittukkoṇṭu, tamatu maṟṟaik kaṇṇaiyuṅ kalliyeṭuttaṟku ampiṉ muṉaiyai ūṉṟiṉār. avvaḷavil avartāñceyta kolait toḻilāl viḷainta tīviṉaiyum oḻintatu; avar muṟṟun tūyarāṉār. karaikaṭanta aṉpiṉāl uḷan tūyarāya kaṇṇapparai mēlun tuṉpuṟutta aruṭperuṅ kaṭalāṉa āṇṭavaṉ tiruvuḷḷam icaiyavillai; uṭaṉē,
nillu kaṇṇappa! nillu kaṇṇappa!
eṉ aṉpuṭait tōṉṟal nillu kaṇṇappa!
eṉa mummuṟai kūṟic civaperumāṉ taṉatu tirukkaiyiṉālē avaratu kaiyaip piṭittut taṭaiceytu avarait taṉatu tiruvaruṭ pēriṉpattiṟ kalappittuk koṇṭaṉaṉ. pārmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! ellā uyirkkun tuṇaivaṉākiya iṟaivaṉ ciṟitum naṭunilai tavaṟāṉāy, aṉpiṟ ṟalainiṉṟa kaṇṇappa nāyaṉāraiyum, avar ciṟṟuyirkaḷaik koṉṟa tīviṉaikku avar tāmē tamatu kaṇṇaik kalliyeṭukkumāṟu ceytum, avvāṟṟāl uyirkkolaiyiṉ tuṉpam ittuṇaik koṭi teṉṟuṇarntu ataṟku avar tāmun tuṉpuṟṟu īṭu koṭukkumāṟu tūṇṭiyum oṟuttu, ataṉ piṉṉarē avarait taṉ tiruvaṭip pēriṉpa vāḻkkaiyil amarvittaṉa ṉeṉṟāṟ, kaṇṇappariṉ karaiyikanta aṉpil ōr aṇuttuṇai tāṉum illāta maṟṟa makkaḷ piṟa uyirkaḷaik koṉṟeṭukkum ūṉaittiṉṟu perukkun tīviṉaikkāka avarait tuṉpuṟuttātu viṭuvaṉō! uyirkkolai ceyvārellām ūṉ tiṉpā rellān tāñ ceyyun tīviṉaikkup piṟavikaṭōṟum aḷaviṟanta tuṉpattai yaṭaintu īṭukoṭuttē tīralvēṇṭum. ivvuṇmai paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷālum,
koṉṟaṉai aṉaittum aṉaittumniṉaik koṉṟaṉa
tiṉṟaṉai aṉaittum aṉaittum niṉait tiṉṟaṉa
eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭamai kāṇka. ivvuṇmai yuṇarātār koṉṟa taṉāl uṇṭākun tīviṉai, koṉṟa uyiriṉ ūṉait tiṉpataṉāṟ pōy viṭum eṉṟu cerukki yuraikkiṉṟārkaḷ! iḵtu aṟiyāmaiyēyākum; ēṉeṉṟāṟ, koṉṟa uyiriṉ ūṉait tiṉpataṉāṟ koṉṟa tīviṉai evvāṟu nīṅkum? oruvaṉaik koṉṟavaṉai ēṉ tūkkiliṭṭuk kollu
kiṟārkaḷ? kolait tuṉpattiṟkuk kolait tuṉpamē īṭākum eṉpatu paṟṟiyaṉṟō? ōr uyiraik koṉṟu ataṉ ūṉait tiṉṟu makiḻpavaṉ, tāṉ ceyta kolait tuṉpattiṟku īṭākat tāṉuṅ kolaittuṉpam aṭaintāṉ āvaṉō! ākavē, ciṟṟuyirkaḷai ūṉiṉ poruṭṭuk kolvā rellām, tām maṟupiṟaviyiṟ ciṟṟuyirkaḷāyp piṟakkat, tām kolaiceyta ac ciṟṟuyirkaḷ makkaḷāyp piṟantu avai tammaik koṉṟu tiṉṟāṟṟāṉ ap pāvam nīṅkum eṉpatē, koṉṟāṟ pāvam tiṉṟāṟpōm eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyiṉ meypporuḷākumeṉṟu aṟimiṉkaḷ! eṉavē, iṟaivaṉ aṉpiṉ mikka taṉ aṭiyavar uyirkkolai ceytirun taṉarāyiṉ, avarai at tīviṉaikkut takkapaṭi tuṉpa īṭu koṭukkac ceytē piṉṉart taṉ tiruvaṭikkaṇ ēṟṟaruḷva ṉeṉpataik kaṭaippiṭit tuṇarmiṉkaḷ! ākavē, kaṇṇappar vilaṅkukaḷaik koṉṟu ūṉaittiṉṟa kuṟṟattai iṟaivaṉ ēṟṟaṉaṉ alla ṉeṉpatūum, ak kuṟṟan tīrum poruṭṭākavē avartamatu kaṇṇait tāmē iṭantu appumāṟu ceytaṉa ṉeṉpatūum aṟintu koḷḷalvēṇṭum.
aḵtokkum, iṟaivaṉai nērē kaṇṭu, mūṉṟāṇṭuc ciṟukuḻaviyā yirunta ñāṉṟē ellām ōtātuṇarntu, aruḷoḻukkattaiyē valiyuṟuttuñ caivacamayattai nilaināṭṭiṉavarāṉa tiruñāṉacampanta mūrtti nāyaṉār tām aruḷicceyta tillaippatikattiṟ paṟappaippaṭut teṅkum pacuvēṭṭu eriyōm puñ, ciṟappar vāḻvatillai eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷil uyirkkolai vēḷvi ceyta tillai vāḻ antaṇaraic ciṟappar eṉṟu uyarttup pāṭiyateṉṉai? ataṉāl, uyirk kolai ceytal kuṟṟam aṉṟeṉpatu avarkkum uṭaṉpāṭupōlu meṉiṉ; aṟṟaṉṟu; aññāṉṟirunta tillaiyantaṇarkaḷ civapirāṉ māṭṭu ellaiyaṟṟa aṉpuṭaiyavarkaḷ. vaṭakkiruntu vanta āriyar ceyta vēḷvikaḷ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākiya civapirāṉai nōkkic ceyyappaṭāmal, intiraṉ varuṇaṉ mittiraṉ mutalāṉa piṟantiṟakkuñ ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷai nōkkiyē ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. atukaṇṭa tillaiyantaṇarkaḷ, avvāriyap pārppaṉarkku nallaṟivu koḷuttum poruṭṭu, avar ceytu pōnta vēḷvikaḷuṭ ciṟantaṉa cilavaṟṟait tāmun taḻuvi, avai tammaic civapirāṉ, oruvar mēlaṉavākavē vaittu vēṭkalāyiṉar; aṅṅaṉam vēṭkumiṭattum, āriyaraip pōl āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṭu māṭu kutirai erumai mutalāṉa uyirkaḷaik kollāmal, ēṉaimakkaḷellām nāṭōṟum uṇavukkākap pallāyirakkaṇakkāyk kollum orucila āṭukaḷaiyē mantiraṅkaḷāl uṇarviḻakkac ceytu koṉṟu vēṭpārāyiṉar. mantiraṅkaḷāl uṇarviḻanta avvāṭukaḷukkut tuṉpa uṇarcci ciṟitumē illaiyākum. iññāṉṟum uṭampiṉ uḷnōy uṭaiyār cilarai aṟuttu nōy tīrkkum maruttuvar, mayakka maruntu oṉṟāl, annōyāḷikaḷai uṇarviḻakkac ceytu, avarkkuc ciṟitumē tuṉpam illāmal avartam uṭampai aṟuttu maruttuvañ ceytal pōlavum, yōka nittirai eṉṉum aṟituyilai varuvikkap paḻakiṉavarkaḷ attuyilai varuvikkum muṟaiyāl aṅṅaṉamē nōyāḷi kaḷaiyum piṟaraiyum uṇarviḻakkac ceytu avartam uṭampukaḷai avarkkuc ciṟitun tuṉpam uṇṭākāmal aṟuttu nōy tīrttal pōlavum, paṇṭaikkālattu antaṇarkaḷ tām vēṭpataṟkuk koṇarvitta vilaṅkukaḷai mantiraṅkaḷāl uṇarviḻakkumāṟu ceytē ataṉpiṉ avaṟṟai veṭṭi vēṭṭaṉar. ivvāṟu vilaṅkukaḷukkuc ciṟitun tuṉpam uṇṭākāvaṇṇam avaṟṟai uṇarviḻakkac ceyyum aṟituyil muṟaiyiṉai (Anaesthesia) nam paṇṭaimakkaḷ aṟittiruntaṉa reṉpatu, irukkuvēta mutal maṇṭilattiṉ, 162 ām patikattil īṟṟil,
nī kaṭavuḷ pāṟcellumpōtu niṉ uyirmūccu
	niṉakkut tiṇaṟutalai uṇṭākkātirukka!
kōṭariyāṉatu niṉ uṭampai ūṟupaṭuttātatāka!
	tiṟamaiyillātu viraintupiḻaipaṭum veṭṭukkāraṉ
		niṉ uṟuppukaḷaip piḻaipaṭap piḷavāṉāka!
uṇmaiyākavē nī iṅkē cākiṉṟilai! nī ētum
	ūṟupaṭṭut tuṉpuṟukiṉṟilai!
nī cīrāṉa eḷiya neṟiyāl tēvarkaḷpāṟ cellu kiṉṟaṉai
eṉap pōnta mantira moḻiyāl naṉku pulaṉākum. naṉṟu coṉṉīr; irukkuvēta patikaṅkaḷ pāṭappaṭṭa attuṇaip paḻaiyakālattē, aṟituyil muṟaikaḷaip paṇṭai makkaḷ aṟintiruntaṉareṉpatu nampaṟpāla taṉṟāmeṉiṟ; kaṭaliṭaiyēyuḷḷa tīvukaḷil vāḻuṅ kāṭṭumirāṇṭi makkaḷum iv vaṟituyil muṟaiyiṉai naṉkaṟintirukkiṉṟaṉareṉa, at tīvukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu avartam vāḻkkaiyiṉ iyalpukaḷai ārāyntu kaṇṭu vanta airōppiya aṟiñarkaḷ eḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.4 nākarika aṟivillāk kāṭavarkaḷum im muṟaiyiṉait terintuḷarāyiṉ, nam mutumakkaḷ itaṉait terintiruntaṉareṉpatiṟ putumaiyum māṟukōḷāvatum ētumillaiyeṉka. iṉi ettakaiya uyirkaḷaiyum aṟituyil muṟaiyil tērccipeṟṟa oruvar oru noṭip poḻutil uṇarviḻakkac ceyyalām eṉpatum, iṉṉum itupōṉṟa aṟituyil nuṭpaṅkaḷum `yōkanittirai eṉṉum ematu nūlil virittu viḷakkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa; avaitammai ataṉkaṭ kaṇṭu koḷka.
ivvāṟu aṟituyil muṟaiyāl orucila āṭukaḷai uṇarviḻakkac ceytu civaperumāṉukku vēṭṭu, avvāṟṟāl āriyar ceytu pōnta pala ciṟu teyvaveṟiyāṭṭu vēḷvikaḷai niṟutti vanta tillaivāḻantaṇar tam aṉpiṉ ceyal pārāṭṭaṟpālatā yiruntamaiyiṉ, tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ avartammai aṅṅaṉañ ciṟappittup pāṭiṉār eṉpatu. aṟṟaṉṟu, āriyar ceytu pōnta kolaimalivēḷviyai niṟutta virumpiya tillaiyantaṇar, tām avaipōṉṟa vēḷvi puriyātiruttalē naṉṟākumaṉṟit, tāmum avarpōṟ ceytal naṉṟāmāṟu yāṅṅaṉameṉiṉ; yāṉai piṭippavaṉ yāṉaiyaik koṇṭē ataṉaip piṭippippaṉ; atupōl, āriyap pārppaṉaraic caiva voḻukkattiṟkut tiruppal vēṇṭiya tamiḻantaṇarum avarpōṟ ṟāmuñ ciṟitu ciṟitu naṭattik kāṭṭiṉālaṉṟi, avar tammait tamvaḻippaṭuttal ākātu; atu kaṇṭē, avar tammai avarōṭu iṉappaṭuttikkoṇṭu avvāṟu ceyyalāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉam ceyyātu viṭṭāl, āriyap pārppaṉar tamiḻantaṇarait tamakku muṟṟum puṟampāṉavarākak karuti, avar kūṟuñ caiva aruḷoḻukkattaik kaippaṟṟātu akaṉṟu oḻukuvar. āriyap pārppaṉartam vēḷvi muṟaikaḷai muṟṟum maṟuttamaiyiṉālallavō pautta camaṇa camayaṅkaḷ puṟaccamayaṅkaḷeṉa avar tammāl aṟavē vilakkappaṭṭu, avaṟṟiṉ ariya aruḷoḻukkak kōṭpāṭukaḷum avar tammāṟ ṟiṉaiyaḷavun taḻuvappaṭātoḻintaṉa; oḻiyavē, appautta camaṇacamayaṅkaḷ innāṭṭil nilaikkaḷaṉ kāṇātum maṟaintu pōyiṉa. ākavē, innuṭpam uṇarnta paṇṭait tamiḻnāṭṭuc cāṉṟōrākiya tamiḻantaṇarkaḷ, āriyap pārp paṉarait tam vaḻippaṭuttutaṟ poruṭṭākavē, avartam vēḷvi muṟaikaḷait tāmun taḻuvuvatu pōṟ ciṟitu ciṟitu ceytu kāṭṭip, piṉ kālañ cellac cellap paṭippaṭiyē evvakai vēḷviyun tamiḻnāṭṭil vēṭkappaṭātapaṭi avaitammai aṟavē niṟuttiviṭṭaṉar. vēḷvi yāṟṟutalai niṟuttiyapiṉ, āriyar tammai viṭṭu vilaki mīṇṭuñ ciṟuteyva vaṇakkattaik kaippaṟṟuvareṉak karutiyē, avar koṇarnta āriyac ciṟu teyvappāṭṭukaḷākiya irukku. yacur, cāmam, atarvam mutaliya vētaṅkaḷait tammuṭaiya civapirāṉ tirukkōyilkaḷil ōtutaṟkum, āriyamoḻic coṟkaḷāṟ civapirāṉai vaḻipaṭutaṟkum, āriyac ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷāṉa intiraṉ varuṇaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷaic civapirāṉ kōyil mūlaikaḷil vaittu vaṇaṅkutaṟkum, tamiḻnāṭṭil nikaḻnta iṟaivaṉ tiruviḷaiyāṭalkaḷaiyum aṭiyār varalāṟukaḷaiyum ellām āriyariṉ vaṭamoḻiyil eḻuti vaittaṟkum, cariyai kiriyai yōkam ñāṉam eṉṉuṅ kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷaiyellām ākamam eṉappeyar tantu vaṭamoḻiyil  eḻutivaittaṟkum oruppaṭṭu niṉṟu, āriyar ceytu pōnta ciṟuteyva vaṇakkattai aṭiyōṭu niṟutti, avarellāñ civapirāṉākiya muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ oṉṟaiyē vaḻipaṭṭuk kolai kuṭi pulāluṇavu nīkki muṟṟuñ caivavoḻukkattiṉarākumpaṭi ceytu, tām koṇṭa mikavuyarnta nalleṇṇattai niṟaivēṟṟiviṭṭaṉar. tuvakkattil mikavum paraparappōṭu virintu paraviya pautta camaṇa camayaṅkaḷiṉ muṉivarkaḷālun tirutta muṭiyāmal, tammait tirutta muṉainta appautta camaṇa camayaṅkaḷaiyum innāṭṭaiviṭṭu ōṭṭiya āriyarait, tamiḻantaṇarkaḷ muṟṟun tirutti avar caiva aruḷoḻukkattaik kaippaṟṟumpaṭi ceytaṉareṉṟāl, nam tamiḻantaṇartam aṟiviṉ nuṭpattaiyum aruḷāṟṟalaiyum eṅṅaṉam eṭuttiyampavallēm! ātalāl, aṉparkaḷē! tillai vāḻantaṇarkaḷ aññāṉṟu vēṭṭa vēḷvikaḷ āriyarait tiruttutaṟporuṭṭuc civapirāṉai nōkkic ceyyappaṭṭa karuttuṇarntē, tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ paṟappaip paṭutteṅkum pacuvēṭ ṭeriyōmpuñ ciṟappar vāḻtillaic ciṟṟampalam eṉṟu avartammai uyarttup pāṭiṉār. pallāyira uyirkaḷaik kākkum poruṭṭu oru cila uyirkaḷaik kollutal kuṟṟam ākāmai araciyal muṟaikkum ottatē yākaliṉ, āriyarvēṭṭa pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa uyirkkolaiyai niṟuttutaṟ karuviyāyt tillai vāḻantaṇar ellām valla iṟaivaṉai nōkkic cilluyir vēṭṭuc ceyta vēḷvikaḷuṅ kuṟṟamākā āyiṉa. āriyar ceṉṟa vaḻiyē ciṟitu tāmuñ ceṉṟu tamiḻantaṇarkaḷ avarait tiruttiṉamaiyiṉālētāṉ, tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa, āriyap pārppaṉar ellāruṅ kolaipulai maṟutta caivavoḻukkat tiṉarāy iṉṟu kāṟum vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟup pauttamum camaṇamum mikap paraviyirunta vaṭanāṭṭiluḷḷa āriyap pārppaṉarō caivavoḻukkañ ciṟitum illārāy iṉṟukāṟum pulāluṇavu koṇṭu kālaṅ kaḻippavarāyc civapirāṉai vaḻipaṭātavarāy irukkiṉṟaṉar; iḵtu etaṉāleṉṟu ārāyntu pārppiṉ, pautta camaṇa mataṅkaḷ āriyaroṭu oruciṟitum ovvātu avarkku muṟṟum māṟāy niṉṟataṉālēyē, āriyar avarai eṭṭuṇaiyum piṉpaṟṟātu tām ceytu pōnta uyirkkolaic ceyalilē nilainiṉṟaṉa reṉpatu pulaṉākum. ivvāṟṟāl āriyarait tiruttiya tamiḻc cāṉṟōriṉ pēraṟivum, avarait tiruttamāṭṭātu tāmum maṟaintoḻinta pautta camaṇa camayattiṉartañ ciṟṟaṟivum evarkkum naṉku viḷaṅkum. eṉavē, tiruñāṉacampantar tillaivāḻantaṇar tam oḻukalāṟṟai uyarttup pēciyatu vāyvatēyāmeṉka.
avvāṟu avar, avartam oḻukkattai uyartturaippiṉum, tām tam vāḻnāḷ ellaiyil eṅkum vēḷvi ceytatumillai; atu ceyyumāṟu piṟarai ēviyatumillai. tam tantaiyār vēḷvi ceyya virumpi ataṉ celavukkākap poruḷ vēṇṭiya kālaiyil, avar kaṭṭa poruḷait tantu avarai viṭuttaṉarēyallāmal, tantaiyār vēṭṭa vēḷvik kaḷattiṟkut tām ceṉṟilar. itaṉāl, vēḷvi vēṭṭalil tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉukkuc ciṟitum uṭampāṭillai yeṉpatu teṟṟeṉap pulaṉātal kāṇka.
iṉik, kōḻi nīrkkōḻi tārā (vāttu) mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ muṭṭaikkum, puṟpūṇṭukaṭkum avaṟṟiṉ payaṉāṉa kāy kaṉi vittuk kiḻaṅkukaṭkum vēṟupāṭu illāmaiyiṉ, muṭṭaiyai yuñ caiva uṇavupporuḷkaḷil oṉṟāka vaittu uṇpatu kuṟṟamākāteṉa orucārār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ārāy taliṉṟi yuraikkum ivaraturai ciṟitum poruttamuṭaittaṉṟu. oru muṭṭaiyāṉatu uruvākāta oru kōḻikkuñcē yākum5 eṉṟu āṅkila iyaṟkaipporuḷ nūlār ārāynturaik kiṉṟamaiyiṉ atu puṟpūṇṭukaḷai yoppatāmeṉṟal aṟiyā vuraiyākum. kōḻiyāl aṭaikākkappaṭum muṭṭaikaḷiliruntu cila nāṭkaḷiṟ kōḻik kuñcukaḷ veḷivarutalai evarum aṟivar. atupōl puṟpūṇṭukaḷaiyum aṭaikākka vaittāl ettaṉai nāḷāyiṉum avaṟṟiliruntu kōḻi nīrkkōḻi tārā mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kuñcukaḷ veḷivarak kaṇṭatuṇṭō? illaiyē. ākaiyāl, muṭṭaikaḷaiyum, puṟpūṇṭukaḷaiyum oṉṟeṉṟal maṭavōruraiyām. mēlum, muṭṭaikaḷiṉ veḷḷaikkaru mañcaṭkaruviṟ puṟpūṇṭukaṭ killā muṭai nāṟṟam iruttalāṉum, akkaruviliruntē cila nāṭkaḷil eṉpu tōl narampu irattam mūḷai mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷellāñ cevvaṉē amainta kōḻikkuñci ṉuṭampu veḷivarak kāṇṭalāṉum, atupōl nilaiyiyaṟ poru ḷākiya puṟpūṇṭukaḷ avaṟṟiṉ payaṉkaḷiliruntu iyaṅkiyaṟ poruḷkaḷākiya kōḻi kuruvi kokku mīṉ āṭu māṭu mutaliyaṉa uṇṭākak kāṇāmaiyālum muṭṭaiyāṉatu caiva uṇavup poruḷkaḷil aṭaṅkumeṉṟal oruvāṟṟāṉum aṭātu. atuvēyumaṉṟic, cila uyirkaṭkup peritu payaṉ paṭumpaṭi iṟaivaṉāl tarappaṭṭa uṭampukaḷ uruvāvataṟku nilaikkaḷaṉākiya muṭṭaikaḷai aḻippatu, avvuyirkaḷiṉ uṭampai aḻippatē yāvatallāṟ piṟitiṉmaiyiṉ, atuvuṅ koṭiya kolaip pāvamēyātal tiṇṇam eṉṟaṟika.
iṉi, iññāṉṟuḷḷārum maruttuvar cilarum muṭṭai tiṉpatu uṭampiṉnalam pēṇuvataṟkuc ciṟantatāmeṉṟu eṇṇuvatuñ colluvatum uṇmaikku māṟupaṭṭaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ēṉeṉṟāl, makkaḷuṭampiṟ cūṭṭiṉait tōṟṟuvittuk koḻuppiṉai vaḷarppataṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum iṉippuṇā (Carbo - hydrates) eṉṉum poruḷ, muṭṭaik karuviṟ ciṟitumē yillai yeṉṟum, narampu narampiṉṟacai mūḷai ciṟu narampukaḷeṉṉum ivaṟṟai valivēṟṟi vaḷarppataṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum mutaluṇā (Protein) eṉṉum poruḷ muṭṭaikkaruvil mikak kuṟaivākavē irukkiṉṟateṉṟum, muṭṭaiyuṇavu tīṉippaiyil eḷitiṟ cerippatillai yeṉṟum6 āṅkila āciriyar naṉkārāyn turaikkiṉṟamaiyiṉ, muṭṭaiyuṇavu makkaḷuṭampukku ēṟṟatallāta tāvatuṭaṉ, aḵtu ōruyiriṉuṭampai aḻikkun tīviṉaiyaiyum uṇṭākkuvatāmeṉṟu ōrntu koḷka. eṉavē, muṭṭaiyuṇavum pulāluṇavēyāy vilakkaṟpālatā meṉka.

mutaṟ patippiṉ mukavurai
makkaḷ nūṟāṇṭu uyirvāḻtal eppaṭi? eṉap peyariya in nūlai iṟṟaikku irupattu nāṉku āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ caumiya vaikāciyil eḻutat tuvaṅkiṉēm. ceṉṟa muppattōrāṇṭukaḷāka emmāl naṭattappaṭṭu varum ematu ñāṉācākaram eṉṉun tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭṭiṉ aintām patumattu mutalitaḻilē itaṉ mutalattiyāyamākiya `nīṇṭa vāḻkkai eṉpatu veḷivarat toṭaṅkiyatu. av vaintām patumattiṉ maṟṟaiyitaḻkaḷilum āṟu, ēḻu, eṭṭu, oṉpatu, pattām patumaṅkaḷiṉ iṭai iṭaiyēyum veḷiyāki vanta itaṉ maṟṟai attiyāyaṅkaḷ. yōka nittirai. maṉita vaciyam eṉṉum emmuṭaiya ēṉai nūlkaḷai appōtu viraintu muṭikka vēṇṭi yeḻunta iṭaiyūṟṟāl ap pattām patumattiṉ 2,3 ām itaḻkaḷil veḷivanta aḷavil niṟka, innūlum iṭaiyē muṭivu peṟātu niṟkalāyiṟṟu. atuvaraiyil veḷivanta in nūliṉ pakkaṅkaḷ 216 ākum, in nūlukku appōtu iṭap paṭṭa peyaril `varuṣam eṉṉum vaṭa coṟ kalantirukkiṉṟatu. innūl mutaṉ mutal eḻutat tuvaṅkiya ñāṉṟu, vaṭacoṟ kalappāl tamiḻmoḻikku nērum pollāṅkiṉai yām uṇarātiruntamaiyāṟ ciṟitēṟak kuṟaiya innūliṉ mutal nūṟu pakkaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟcila vaṭacoṟkaḷ iṭaiyiṭaiyē kalantuviṭalāyiṉa.
ñāṉacākarap pattām patumattu 3ām itaḻil niṉṟupōya in nūṟpakuti mīṇṭum ñāṉacākaram patiṉain tām patumattu 3 āmitaḻilē tuvakkañ ceyyappaṭṭuc ciṟitēṟakkuṟaiyat toṭarpāka ataṉ kaṇṇum. patiṉāṟām patumattu mutal nāṉkitaḻkaḷilum veḷiyāki. ippōtu appatumattiṉ iv 5,6ām itaḻkaḷil muṭivu peṟalāyiṟṟu. iṭaiyilē māṇikkavācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum  eṉṉum periya nūlai ñāṉacākaram 10ām patumattiṉ 10ām itaḻiliruntu tuvaṅki ēḻāṇṭukaḷ peritārāynteḻuti, 14ām patumattiṉ īṟṟil muṭikka vēṇṭiya kaṭṭāyam nērntamaiyāl, innūl ciṟitēṟak kuṟaiya eṭṭāṇṭukaḷ varaiyil iṭaiyē eḻutap paṭutaṟku iṭamillāmal niṉṟu pōyatu. eṉṟālum, ellām valla iṟaivaṉ tiruvaruḷāl ip perunūl irupattu nāṉku āṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉarēṉum ippōtu iṉitu muṭivupeṟa lāṉamaikku ematu pulliya vaṇakkamem perumāṉ ṟiruvaṭip pōtukaṭku uriyatākuka!
in nūlai muṭittaṟku ittaṉai kālañ ceṉṟa tāyiṉum, itiṟ collappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaiyum muṟai kaḷaiyum paṟṟiyām ōvātu ārāyntapaṭiyākavēyirun tēm. nōy varā vakaikaḷaiyum vanta nōy nīkkumuṟai kaḷaiyum paṟṟittamiḻ āṅkila vaṭamoḻikaḷiṟ cāṉṟōr eḻutiya uyarnta nūlkaḷ paṟpalavaṟṟai yām payiṉṟaṟinta vaḷavil nillātu, avai tammai yellām emmiṭattē ceytu pārttum. emmaic cārntāriṭattē ceytu pārttum. avaṟṟuḷ evai mikac ciṟantaṉavāyc celavillaṉavāy eḷitiṟ ceytu payaṉpeṟat takkaṉavāyt teḷiyappaṭṭaṉa vō avaikaḷai in nūliṉkaṇ varain tirukkiṉṟēm.
ittakaiyatoru payaṉ ciṟanta urainūl tamiḻ moḻikkaṇ illāp peruṅkuṟaiyāl, āṇ peṇ pālāriṟ perumpakutiyiṉar tamatu vāḻkkai yiṟ piḻaimika oḻukip palvakai nōykaḷālum poruṭcelavālum vaṟumaiyālum piṭiyuṇṭu, tām āṇṭu mutirā muṉṉarē iṟantoḻiva tallāmalun. tam vayiṟṟiṟ piṟakkum piḷḷaikaḷum. avarkkup piṟakkum piḷḷaikaḷum ellām eṅṅaṉamē peruntuṉpaṅkaḷukku vaḻivaḻi āḷāki maṭintu pōkavuñ ceytuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ tuṉpam mikunta ik kuṟu vāḻkkaiyaik kaṇṭu ematuḷḷattil uṇṭāṉa irakkamē in nūlaiyiyaṟṟi veḷiyiṭumāṟu emmait tūṇṭalāyiṟṟu. makkaḷ vāḻkkaiyaip paṟṟiya in nūlil, entap pakutiyaiyum maṟaitteḻututal ākāmaiyālum. aṉṟi aṅṅaṉam maṟait teḻutiṉāl, iṉṟiyamaiyātu terintu oḻuka vēṇṭiya muṟaikaḷ viṭappaṭṭu. ataṉāl āṇ peṇ pālārai iḵtu aṟiyāmaiyiṉiṉ ṟeṭukkamāṭṭātāyp payaṉiṉṟik kaḻiyumātalālum `āṇpeṇ cērkkai, `makkaṭpēṟu `karu vilakku mutalāṉa pakutikaḷiṟ colla vēṇṭuvaṉa vellām oru ciṟitum maṟaiyāmal tiṟappākavē varain tirukkiṉṟēm. makkaḷ vāḻkkaiyiṉ uyirnilaiyāyuḷḷa ic ciṟanta poruḷ kaḷait teḷintu viḷakkātu viṭutalāl vaḻi vaḻip perukum peruntīṅkiṉai naṉkuṇarntē. tirumūlar mutalāṉa nam paṇṭaiyāciriyarkaḷum ivai tammait tam nūlil maṟaiyā teṭuttu viḷakki aṟivuṟut tirukkiṉṟaṉar. iññāṉṟai āṅkila amerikka āciriyar kaḷiṟ pukaḻ mikappaṭaitta āṭavarum makaḷiruṅkūṭa ittuṟaikaḷil maṟaivu ciṟitu miṉṟiyē tiṟanteḻuti varumāṟṟai, itaṟkup piṉṉuḷḷa ematu āṅkila mukavurai yuḷḷum eṭuttup pēciyiruk kiṉṟēm. ātalāl vāḻkkai yiṉ maṟaiporuḷ nuṭpaṅkaḷ muṟṟum, nōy nīkkum iyaṟkai muṟaikaḷ muṟṟum naṉkeṭuttu viḷakkum innūl āṇpeṇpālār irutiṟattiṉarkkum peritu payaṉ paṭṭu avarai nīṭiṉitu vāḻvikkum eṉṉum nampikkai mikutiyum uṭaiyēm.
innūliṉ mutaṟpakutiyil viraviyirukkuñ ciṟcila vaṭacoṟkaḷaiyum itaṉ iraṇṭām patippiṉkaṭ kaḷaintu viṭutaṟku tiruvaruḷ viraiviṟ kūṭṭuvatāka.

tiruvaḷḷuvar āṇṭu, 1963	maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ
mācittiṅkaḷ, 3 ām nāḷ

Preface
“On  a natural system of diet, old age would be our last and our only malady; he term of our existence would be protracted; we should enjoy life and no longer preclude others from the enjoyment of it.”
- Poet Shelly in “A Vindication of Natural Diet”
The following second large work of mine (my first large work being “The Life and Times of St Manickavachakar) in Tamil embodies the results of my careful study of Long Life problems, of my observations of symptoms in myself and in others in health and in disease, and of my experimental modes of living an dmethods of healing pursued continually for more than thirty years. Nothing but what has been found in all these thirty years  to benefit others and myself is set down in the following pages. Before recommending to others what I read and thought about how to maintain a healthy life and how to restore it in cases where it was impaired, I tried them all on myself and on my people undergoing of course considerable trouble and expending large sums of money all through, until I came to hit upon the very simple, natural, correct, inexpensive modes of leading and maintaining a healthy long life. Although it took me twenty four years to complete this treatise owing to interruptions occasioned by my other literary, religious and historical works, yet it has gained - much in my long varied experince, wide study and progressive thinking.
Here in India as elsewhere, it is generally believed at living long does not lie within the power of man. Most of the people in India attribute to the power of late  both a short life and a long one. Only a few hold to the view that man has infinite possibilities in store, if he would only care to exercise his will, and that according as he energizes it or leaves it dormant, does he either lengthen it or leaves it dormant, does he either lengthen his life’s duration or shorten it to a span. These few are mostly the adherente of the yoga system of philosophy. In ancient times not only but so in Greece, the followers  of Kapila and Patanjali, and Pythagoros practised strict yogic modes of living and thinking and eventually enjoyed a long life extending to hundred years and more.
	But in recent times when the practice of yoga and therewith the natural ways of living and the loftier ways of thinking had the more fallen into disuse, as the people became more and more illiterate, ignorant and aggressively selfish indulging in animal passions and aggressively selfish indulging in animal passions and morbidly craving fpr innatural fllds and drinks, the human life also has become hideously short. Most horrible forms of disease have multiplied and great sufferings and countless premature deaths do occur. Still they pass unnoticed simply as ineviable occurrences. The yogic mode of living has even come to be looked upon as belonging exclusively to the ascetic few.
	But, towards the end of the last century Saint amalinga in Southern India and Saint RamaKrishna and his great and illustrious disciple Swami Vivekananda in Northern India had, by their impressive teachings and thrilling lectures, revived the study and practice of Yoga to an appreciable degree and showed how all who are willing might share in the benefits of Yoga.
	Still earlier in far- off countries Newton the great discoverer  of scientific laws, struck deeply by the fact lf the wonderfully long life led by some men-especially by Thlmas Parr who lived 153 years, and Henry Jenkins 169, devoted his attention to a study of the habits and diets of the long lived persons and found out the great law existing between the purity and simplicity of the former and the long life of the latter. He then wrote a “luminous and the eloquent essay” on Defence of Vegetable Regimen expounding the principles that underlie the perpetuation of life, and thus gave a new impetus to the investigation  of laws governing the length of human life. After the time of Newton, the question of clean food and its intimate relation to a healthy long life, touched the finer feelings of so great an  angelic soul as the poet Shelley and so much excited his compassion for human and animal life that he promptly wrote a thoughtful, sound and brilliant essay entitled. “ A Vindication of Natural Diet” and disseminated his precious ideas all over the foreign countries.
	From the time of Shelly this important subject began to engage the attention of many great scientists such as Louis Kuhne, Dr. A. Haig, Prof. Metchnikoff, Prof. Loeb, Dr. Albert Matthews, and Dr. J. H. Kellogg and of such great thinkers and philosophers as Prentice Mulford, Helen Wilmans, Harry Gaze and others. Dr. Haig’s book on “Uric Acid” and Dr. Kellogg’s “The New Dietetics” are teally epoch-making works in as much as they bring to clear light the hitherto hidden fact relating to food and life. The number of learned people who are greatly benefited by the teachings of these eminent authorities is daily increasing by tens and hundreds of thousands in foreign countries, while here in Southern India those who know even the names of these savants are extremely rare.
	When this vital knowledge is in such a deplorable condition even among the English-educated people of this country, is it to be wondered at for its being mjch more so among others who are almost illiterate and who only speak Tamil but know not either to read or write any thing in it? That the increasing number of the English- educated persons should, after completing their university course, go hunting after posts in Government Offices and ilsewhere, mostly not with any honourable intention but with an avidity to scrape up as much as they can the people’s money, that they should vie with each other in spending it as much in adorning their females with costly diamond jewels and silk cloths of every high grade as in other vain luxuries, that they should take no thought of the poor, suffering and prematurely dying millions in the country, of their language and religion and their social amelioration, is really a heart-rending spectacle. 
It was this lamentable state of affairs that at first roused in me a feeling of boldness to layout the limited sources of my income in the acquisition and propagation of knowledge pertaining to the science of health, although this to a certain extent interfered with my study of English, Tamil and Sanscrit literatues, of the different systems of philosophy and different religious systems of the world. Even though the following work gives an elaboratge treatment to health - subjects, yet it is not done in the cold light of purposeless materialistic science. On the other hand, ideas common to allreligions and philosophies are brought to enliven and ennoble the subject and I trust that, after reading it once, one would find his or her mind greatly refreshed and exalted.
A work of this kind is quite new to the Tamil people and it may even seem strange to many a seemingly religious person that it should give such an explicit treatment to such avoided subjects as those that concern sex-functions and birth-control. Perceiving the evil of concealing such vital matters from the knowledge of people, even women of high attainments, and even men of great religious distinctions have, in the foreign countries, come forward to speak and write on those subjects without any reserve. Marie Stopes, and E.A. Rout’s deservingly famous writings are familiar instances in point. And there is not a greater and stronger upholder of the needs of birh-control than the illustrious Dean Inge of St. Paul’s, nor is there a deeper and more logical thinker and exponent of the science of eugenics than Dr. F.C.S. Sehiller of Oxford. “Eugenists believe” says Dean Inge “that unless civilisation is guided on scientific principles, it must come to ruin. We are ready to give up all our theories, if we can be proved to be in the wrong; but we stand by scientific as against emotional or sentimental ethics” (outspoken Essays, Second  Series). And Dr. Chiller lays greater emphasis on this fact by affirming However powerful, therefore, a society may be, and however great its resources, it is doomed if it so organizes itself is to breed the wrong sort of men and to favour the survival of the worthless at the expense of the more valuable” (Eugenics and Politics).
Ages ago our own country was not wanting in wise men who perceived the evil of indiscriminate procreation. St. Thirumoolar who existed in the 5th century A.D., not only speaks of what ought to be the right sort of sex - functions, but also indicates the conditions under which a limited number of choice offspring is producible. From this it just be clear that, in treating explicitly of sexual functions and birth - control methods, I have not don4e anything offensive against delicacy of taste but have simply voiced forth that the great men of the past and the present thought and aid on the subject.
Another prominent feature of this work is its strong advocacy of nature - cure methods. If man can learn to make a proper use of air, light, heat, water and dwelling places, if he can live only upon such vegetable foods is contain all the elements required for the building up of his body, if he can keep clean the excretory organs without allowing the used - up matter to remain in them long, if he can fast occasionally so as to give rest to his over-wrought organ the stomach, if he can keep his blood free from impurities and contamination, and lastly if he can exercise his mind and body to an extent beneficent but not fatiguing, there is no reason why he should not live a helahty long life and why he should abandon the use of medicines altogether. One who has acquired the habit of living according to the principles laid down in this book need no longer fear either disease or untimely death. If, by unforeseen circumstances, anything goes wrong with him, all that he must do is simply to adopt the simple, inexpensive and natural methods of healing shown in the last chapters of this book and the cure will positively follow as a matter of course. With these few words  I send out this most useful work of mine for the benefit of the Tamil people and offer my humble prayer to God for having enabled me to complete it after so long.
The Sacred Order of Love,	Vedachalam
Pallavaram,
13th Feb. 1933.
